Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on nlp structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 163. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 141 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the nlp decomposition. By minimality, the element a^163 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 141-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The nlp variety V defined by structural decomposition constraints admits a stratification into 851 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 204 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 851 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the nlp structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 204. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
